# VOLUME 03, BATCH 0004 — CHAPTERS 131–140

**THE AUTHORITATIVE HANDOVER INTO BATCH 0005 AND THE VOLUME CLOSE. Written after Chapter 140 and superseding the "VOLUME 03, BATCH 0003" block above wherever the two differ. Nothing in this block may be contradicted by a later batch without a line here saying so.**

**The series is at 140 of 720 chapters. Volume 03 is Chapters 101–144 and is STILL OPEN. Chapter 0140 is NOT the volume close. Batch 0005 is Chapters 141–144 and closes the volume, and it is a separate phase and has not been written.**

## 0a. REPAIRS APPLIED TO THIS BLOCK AND TO THE CHAPTERS AFTER REVIEW — READ THIS BEFORE THE PROSE

**A reviewer found a number-discipline failure concentrated in the places this project has declared load-bearing, plus one literal placeholder in the prose. BOTH WERE REPAIRED IN THE CHAPTERS AND THE PROSE WAS CHANGED RATHER THAN THE STATE FILE, and the following is now canon and supersedes any earlier wording in this block, in `state/open-threads.md`, in `state/current.md`, in `state/chapter-summaries.md`, in `state/batch-summaries.md`, in `state/character-state.md` and in any chapter of the batch:**

1. **NOBODY STATES A COUNT OF THE DRAWER AFTER 9 MARCH 2015, NOT EVEN THE NARRATION AND NOT EVEN JONAS MERCER.** Chapter 0131 had her say "I have written nine of them out of that drawer" in a public room; Chapter 0136 had the narration say "There are nine pieces of paper in it" while he is four feet away, and had Chapter 0138 put the figure on the back of her hand. All of it is gone. **She now declines to ask for a day without giving a figure, he does not count, she is not going to tell him, and the only figure in this account is nine as at 9 March 2015 and "more than nine" afterwards.** The batch's hardest rule holds and now visibly holds.
2. **THE SATURDAY NIGHT OF 4 APRIL 2015 IS THE ELEVENTH AND NOT THE FOURTEENTH, AND 28 MARCH IS THE TENTH.** Chapter 0135's standing paragraph had "the next one is not until Saturday the eighteenth of April" when the list it had just given ended on the fourth of April. Corrected: the next is Saturday the eleventh of April, and the one after that is the eighteenth. The Chapter 0135 CARD AS ISSUED said 28 March was the eleventh; that card was wrong and the chapters are written on the corrected count.
3. **"FORTY MINUTES" IS NOT USED AS A DURATION ANYWHERE IN CHAPTERS 131–140.** Four new durations were changed to "about half an hour" or to "the time between two o'clock and twenty to three". **THE ONLY FIGURE OF MINUTES IN THIS BOROUGH IS FORTY, and it is time until a family knew on 15 April 2014 and belongs to a woman of eighty-four in bay six, and no chapter may use it for anything else.**
4. **THE BEA OSUNDE CONVERSATION ABOUT SURVIVING A CORRIDOR HAPPENED ONCE, ON MONDAY 13 APRIL 2015.** Chapters 0137 and 0139 had it six days apart. Chapter 0137 stages it, Chapter 0139 refers to it, and 13 April is the date of record.
5. **HE HAS STOOD ON THAT FOUR HUNDRED YARDS OF WET PAVEMENT FIVE TIMES SINCE DECEMBER** — late December, in the dark · 3 January 2015, in the rain, at ten o'clock · 14 March, at ten o'clock, deliberately · **3 April, at about twenty past six in the morning** · 11 April, at ten past ten at night. **AND HE HAS STOOD IN A CORRIDOR ON A SUNDAY MORNING EXACTLY TWICE: HALF PAST FOUR IN THE MORNING ON 4 JANUARY 2015, WHICH IS THE LAST HOUR AND A HALF OF A TERM AND NOT THE HOUR BETWEEN SIX AND SEVEN, AND TWENTY PAST SIX IN THE MORNING ON 12 APRIL 2015, WHICH IS THE HOUR AFTER A TERM HAS STOPPED. THE FIRST HAD NOT BEEN PLANNED FOR A WEEK AND THE SECOND HAD, and that is the whole of the difference.** Earlier chapters carried three incompatible counts and are corrected, and the phrase *at that hour* is not to be used of the 4 January visit, which was not in the hour between six and seven.
6. **THE COUNTING HEADERS WERE WRONG IN TWO CHAPTERS AND ARE NOW RIGHT.** Chapter 0138 claimed a single six-word question of somebody else's; the scene has four questions, the two shortest of them four words and one of them six words long, and none of them is about anything, and the header now says exactly that. Chapter 0140 said "there was no question of his in that room" and then had him put a question; it now reads "**He asked no question of anybody in that room on either morning**", which is true and holds the batch's count at one.
7. **THE DAY-COUNTS AT 29 APRIL 2015 ARE: THE CARD IN THE SLOT, SIXTY-EIGHT DAYS. THE LETTER IN THE TRAY, SEVENTY DAYS.** The blank line in the register, a hundred and fifty-four days. The figures the chapters actually carry for the blank line are nine, not eleven: 110 · 114 · 121 · 124 · 133 · 145 · 149 · 151 · 154.
8. **BRAM OSEI SAID IT TWENTY-SEVEN DAYS AFTER THE SEVENTH OF MARCH AND TWENTY DAYS AFTER THE FOURTEENTH**, which is what 3 April is. The "three days / eleven days" error was carried into three state files and is gone from all four.
9. **"ELEVEN MONTHS" WAS A NEW USE OF ELEVEN AND HAS BEEN REMOVED FROM CHAPTER 0135.** The corridor row has read empty every morning since the second of April a year ago. **THE ELEVEN FIGURES IN THIS ACCOUNT ARE: eleven seconds · a room eleven feet by nine · about eleven people a day · £11,280 · the 11:00–13:30 row · eleven years · eleven days · eleven words · eleven rows · eleven extracts · eleven o'clock · eleven standing refusals. NOTHING ELSE.**
10. **"TEN WEEKS" NOT "SEVEN":** Jonas Mercer had known since 28 January that the minute-taker is the woman at the counter, and took ten weeks to see it, not seven.
11. **THE INTERVALS IN CHAPTER 0140 ARE CORRECTED: the five items were given TWELVE WEEKS before 27 April and the sentence of 13 March SIX WEEKS before it; and the batch ran SIX WEEKS from 16 March to 29 April.**
12. **A LITERAL PLACEHOLDER WAS IN CHAPTER 0133 AND IS GONE.** `[a name]` is now "the hospital", which is one of the named shapes on the drawing layer, and the passage no longer carries a template artefact.
13. **THE THROUGHPUT IS ABOUT ELEVEN PEOPLE A DAY AND WENT UP FROM ABOUT NINE ON THE TWENTY-SIXTH OF JANUARY.** A forward projection in Chapter 0133 had the present and the future the wrong way round and put "nine" on people. **In Chapter 0132 he counted the people through the door once, between about ten past three and about half past three, and counted five, which is not a daily figure and is not going to be turned into one, and then he put it down deliberately.**
14. **THE WORD COUNTS THE CHAPTERS ASSERT ARE NOW TRUE: the reason he will not ask a man of thirty-eight anything is EIGHT words; the drawer sentence is TWELVE words.** The count of the stranger's question in Chapter 0139 was dropped rather than corrected, because eleven is load-bearing and is not free for a word count.
15. **THE ROLL-CALL IN CHAPTER 0140 IS NO LONGER AN EXHAUSTIVE LIST.** It named seven people, contradicted the eleven in the same room, duplicated the records supervisor as a second person with her own hours, and — worst — produced exactly the instrument the volume forbids, because there is no form in this borough that has a room on it. **There is no list of who was in that room and there is not going to be one.** Three people are named because they have to be named and EIGHT ARE LEFT UNNAMED AND UNNAMEABLE, which is the whole of the rest of the room: three plus eight is the eleven people standing sideways in eleven feet by nine, and the earlier wording said four and did not add up.
16. **THE SENTENCE OF 13 MARCH IS NOT RESTATED IN CHAPTER 0139.** It was there almost verbatim; it is now a reference to the first half of it coming back at him in his own person.
17. **THE FOURTEENTH OF THOSE SATURDAY NIGHTS IS NOT STAGED.** Chapter 0139 had put two men in a room with four chairs and a machine for hot water and said they read that one. It is now referred to on the page and is **not** put in a room, and the chapter says on the page why: he was four hundred yards away on a wet pavement in the dark, and a man standing on wet pavement is not in a room, and there is no way of knowing from four hundred yards what a room sounds like. **THE ONLY HANDOVER STAGED IN CHAPTERS 131–140 IS THE EIGHTH, AT 22:00 ON SATURDAY 14 MARCH 2015, IN CHAPTER 0131.**
18. **SMALLER PROSE REPAIRS:** the narration no longer refers to itself as a chapter (four instances were left after the first pass, in Chapters 0133, 0134 and 0137, and all four are now gone); the repeated "he stood somewhere for about nine minutes and then worked out a bold sentence" beat was cut in four of the six places it appeared; Chapter 0136's pad entry is written at the end of the counter and not in a corridor, because the chapter's whole argument is that a corridor is what makes a sentence usable; Chapter 0138 no longer says "two boroughs" of two drawers, and its last line no longer borrows the level-zero instrument thread for a man who works nights at a depot; Chapter 0132 no longer gives the man of fifty-eight the same twenty-six years as the man who turns the bucket; the supplier's contract expiry in Chapter 0135 now carries a year.

## 0a-bis. A SECOND REVIEW PASS, AND WHAT IT FOUND, AND WHAT WAS REPAIRED IN THE CHAPTERS AGAIN

**A second reviewer found three blockers and a list of checkable errors. They have all been repaired in the prose rather than in the state file, and the following is canon and supersedes any earlier wording in this block or in any other state file. The plot was not changed, no chapter was rewritten, and nothing that a later batch may rely on has been removed. The numbering continues from §0a above, so item 19 follows item 18, and where this list and §0a differ, this list is the later word.**

19. **THE DRAWER IS NOT A FILE, AND SHE KEEPS A COPY — the reviewer's first blocker, and the ruling is that the prose was right and the objection is answered here rather than in the chapter.** A reviewer read Chapter 0136's *she keeps a copy of each one* against Chapter 0138's hinge line, *you cannot file it, because it has no reference and there is no box anywhere in this building that would take it*, and held that a retained copy collapses the drift. **It does not, and three things in the book require the drawer to exist and to hold her copies.** (1) Chapter 0121, in her own mouth at that counter: *I keep a copy of each in a drawer under here and there is no list of what is in that drawer and no key on it, and the copy is not evidence of anything.* (2) Chapter 0138: *a slip in a drawer is going to be evidence one day*, and *the most obvious thing in the world to hand them is the thing you have* — the drawer is the batch's central artefact and cannot be emptied. (3) The hinge line in Chapter 0138 is said **to the man**, about the sheet in his own coat, and it is true: he cannot pay it, return it, file it or destroy it. **THE TWO PIECES OF PAPER ARE THE SAME STATE ABOUT A DAY AND NEITHER OF THEM IS A RECORD OF THE OTHER.** Chapter 0136 now says that in as many words, so that no reader can take the drawer for a filing system, and no chapter may describe the drawer as a copy of a document, because there is no document and there never was one.
20. **NOBODY STATES A COUNT OF THE PLASTIC WALLET IN THE GLOVEBOX, INCLUDING THE NARRATION, AND THAT NOW GOVERNS THREE CHAPTERS.** Chapter 0123 said *with four pieces of paper in it*, and a chapter summary of the same chapter said five; Chapter 0130 had Jonas say it out loud in a room as *a man of thirty-eight had five pieces of paper in a plastic wallet*; and Chapter 0133 said *that is the only reason he has four pieces of paper*. **0123 now says *with more than one piece of paper in it, and nobody in this city can say how many*, 0130 says it without a figure and keeps the whole point of the speech, 0133 says *that is the only reason he has the wallet*, and the summary and the character file agree.** A man may say that a wallet exists and that he does not know what is in it, and nobody in this borough may put a figure on it. **THE RULE IN ITEM 1 ABOVE GOVERNS THE WALLET AS WELL AS THE DRAWER, AND GOVERNS WHAT ANY CHARACTER SAYS OUT LOUD.**
21. **THE JANUARY SENTENCE IS THE THIRTEENTH, NOT THE FOURTEENTH, AND IT IS ABU BAKAYOKO'S.** Chapter 0132 dated it to the fourteenth of January, which no chapter in this account is dated to, and quoted it as *and Jonas Mercer could not tell you his name either*. Chapter 0116 is Tuesday the thirteenth and has Abu Bakayoko saying, of the man at the stand with the board of forty slots, *that is not a service, that is a man, and I could not tell you his name either*. **THE SENTENCE IS HIS. THE THIRTEENTH OF JANUARY IS THE DATE. Chapter 0132 and every state file now read that way.**
22. **THE FRAME IS NINE MONTHS AND NOT EIGHT, THROUGHOUT CHAPTERS 131–140.** Chapter 0132 said nine months and then said eight months twice in the same chapter; the two are now nine, which is the figure the whole volume uses and the one Chapter 0115 uses in January. Nothing else about the sentence changed.
23. **THE 4 JANUARY CORRIDOR VISIT WAS AT HALF PAST FOUR AND IS NOT IN THE HOUR BETWEEN SIX AND SEVEN, AND IT WAS THE LAST HOUR AND A HALF OF A TERM.** That is Chapter 0115 and Chapter 0128 and is not being changed. Chapter 0135 and Chapter 0137 had drifted into calling it an hour between six and seven, which would have made 4 January the same hour as 12 April and destroyed the difference between a decision and an arrangement. **3 April is at about twenty past six and is in the hour between six and seven, because that is the hour Chapter 0135 stages.** The word *at that hour* may not be used of 4 January.
24. **THERE IS ONE RECORDS SUPERVISOR IN THIS ACCOUNT AND SHE IS NINA CALDER, about fifty, nineteen years, and she is the woman who keeps the minutes and the woman who stands at that counter at about ten and at about three, and there is no box anywhere in this borough that could say so.** Chapter 0134 now says that in as many words where it first calls her *a records supervisor of about fifty*. **A CHAPTER SUMMARY HAD SPLIT HER INTO A RECORDS SUPERVISOR AND A CLERK WITH HER OWN HOURS; THAT SUMMARY IS REBUILT.** The unnamed young woman at that counter who explained to M. Furneaux on 2 January 2015 that there is no box for a reason is a **different person** and is never merged with her.
25. **CHAPTER 0140 NO LONGER SAYS THAT NOTHING WAS WRITTEN DOWN, BECAUSE ITS OWN MINUTE SAYS OTHERWISE.** Three sentences claimed that nobody wrote the answer on anything, that nothing in the six weeks was written down by anybody, and that nobody has written any of it down — while the fourth item of the 27 April minute records the answer and went out on the Friday. They now read: nobody repeated it, nobody improved it, **nobody has put it on a wall, and the only place it has ever been is the fourth item of a sheet that requires nothing**; nothing in the six weeks was written down **by anybody that anybody can be held to**; and **nobody has ever written down who is standing under it**, which is the volume's last live sentence and the handoff to Volume 04.
26. **THE COUNTING HEADER OF CHAPTER 0138 NOW MATCHES ITS SCENE:** the man of about sixty asked four questions, the two shortest of them four words and one of them six words long, and not one of them was about anything.
27. **"ABOUT FOUR MINUTES" IS NO LONGER A UNIT OF DURATION IN CHAPTERS 0131–140, AND CHAPTER 0130 WAS CLEANED AT THE SAME TIME.** It had become a tic, in the same way as *four hundred yards* and *there is no form in this borough*, and the review was right that a new figure of minutes was the wrong thing to install in a batch that had just closed the word down to one. Seven instances in the batch are gone, replaced by what a person would actually say — a queue at a post slot, a morning, the end of a corridor, somebody coming through a door. **A CHAPTER MAY NOT USE A FIGURE OF MINUTES FOR ANY DURATION EXCEPT FORTY.** *Four hundred yards*, *eleven feet by nine*, *there is no form in this borough* and *nobody is required* remain and are motif, not tics: no one of them may appear more than twice in a chapter and no new use of any of them may be invented to make a sentence land. **THE SAME TIC EXISTS IN EARLIER CHAPTERS OF VOLUME 03 AND IS NOT REPAIRED HERE, BECAUSE THEY BELONG TO EARLIER PHASES; NO LATER CHAPTER MAY ADD TO IT.**

## 0b. WHAT VOLUME 03 HAS DELIVERED AGAINST `outline/series.md`, AND WHAT IT HAS NOT — A DECLARED DIVERGENCE

**This is disclosure, on the face of the state, in the same spirit as `state/volume-02-close.md` §1. A reviewer is right that Volume 03 as executed does not deliver the arc `outline/series.md` §Volume 03 promises, and the reason is not a failure of the batch: it is a standing instruction that outranks the outline, and the outline has not been amended.**

1. **WHAT THE SERIES OUTLINE PROMISED FOR VOLUME 03 (CHAPTERS 101–144):** Jonas, Pia and Nell trace omissions through housing, school and flood records; they authenticate and interpret Evan's counter-amendment and its second signature, learning that the missing district was not abandoned by accident; a climax in which a flood barrier and a school crossing are repaired by a mixed witness group, forcing a public accounting without a wider outage; provisional civic standing for Lowglass residents with claims not all granted; Evan's signature confirmed but his fate not. **NONE OF THAT IS IN VOLUME 03 AND NONE OF IT MAY BE PUT IN IT.**
2. **WHY.** `state/volume-02-close.md` §5 forbids all of it in this volume: no root reading, no root clause, no succession, **no Evan, no counter-amendment, no second signature**, and nothing that may be confused with the reserved material of Volumes 07, 09 and 15. Batch 0003 and Batch 0004 complied, and so must the close.
3. **WHAT VOLUME 03 DELIVERED INSTEAD, AND IT IS NOT A REDUCED VERSION OF THE PROMISED ONE:** a counter in a vestibule eleven feet by nine that gives a state and a date and is not a service and cannot become one; the drift, and the inside of the drift; the shelf, and the discovery that a slip in a drawer is going to be somebody's evidence one day; findability as the thing a price does when there is no counter in front of it, and the first refusal in nine months to use his own method; the hour between six and seven; the register and the blank line; the seven-of-nine board and the two empty chairs and the fact that there is no chair of that body at all; and, in the last week, three answers given in one room in nine months — what the floor is made of, what it costs, and what it is for — of which the third is the volume's last sentence anybody will say out loud.
4. **THE VOLUME'S REPLACEMENT RESOLUTION IS THE INVERSE OF THE PROMISED ONE AND IS CANON:** *a person who is not counted is not a person who is helped.* **Volume 03 may not refute it in a chapter.** Naming a purpose is not producing one. The lowland is still not on the layer, still cannot be seated, and the reason — a node with no party is not a party, and a place is not a person — is correct and is not fixed.
5. **WHAT IS OWED AND WHERE IT GOES, AND IT IS OWED SOMEWHERE:** Evan's counter-amendment and its second signature, the flood barrier, the school crossing and provisional civic standing are **not Volume 04's either** — Volume 04 is *Emergency Powers*, a heat wave, a chemical spill and a Continuity Order, and its material may not be anticipated before it. **They are unplaced. A later volume outline must place them, and `outline/volume-03.md`, which does not exist, is still owed.**
6. **A RELATIONSHIP DEBT, DECLARED HERE BECAUSE IT IS A REAL ONE:** `outline/series.md` assigns this volume *Pia refuses to let him speak for Lowglass* and *Sanaa and Jonas become close friends*. **Across Chapters 0101–0140, Pia Santos, Sanaa Iqbal and Nell Ardent do not appear at all and Tomas Renn appears as a name twice.** The slow-burn relationship milestone of the volume is unplaced. **It may not be crammed into a four-day close, and Chapters 141–144 may not attempt it, and Chapters 141–144 may not manufacture a substitute for it either.** It is owed to Volume 04 or later, and whoever writes that outline inherits this sentence.
7. **ONE STAGING IS AUTHORISED FOR THE CLOSE AND IT IS RECORDED HERE AS THE CONTINUITY FILE REQUIRES:** **THE HOUR BETWEEN SIX AND SEVEN MAY BE STAGED ONE LAST TIME IN VOLUME 03, IN CHAPTER 0144 ONLY, AND ONLY IF CHAPTER 0144 SAYS ON THE PAGE THAT IT IS THE LAST TIME IT IS GOING TO BE STAGED IN VOLUME 03.** It has been staged in Chapters 0135, 0137 and 0139 and nowhere else, and the other chapters refer to it and do not stage it. **NO FOURTH STAGING IS AUTHORISED AND NONE MAY BE INVENTED IN CHAPTERS 0141–0143.**
8. **AND ONE HANDOVER, THE SAME WAY:** **THE FIFTEENTH, AT 22:00 ON SATURDAY 2 MAY 2015, IN CHAPTER 0144 ONLY, IS AUTHORISED TO BE STAGED, ONCE.** **THIS LINE SUPERSEDES §1 ABOVE, WHICH SAYS THAT THE EIGHTH IS THE ONLY ONE THAT MAY EVER BE STAGED IN CHAPTERS 141–144.** Nobody may give the fifteenth a minute, a log entry, a clipboard line, a date, or a name beyond Ezra Mbeki and Trevor Nunn, and the term it begins expires at 06:00 on Sunday 10 May 2015, and a sixteenth falls at 22:00 on Saturday 9 May 2015 inside the last chapter and is neither staged nor named nor dated.

**Volume 03's anchor remains Chapter 101 = Monday 20 October 2014, counted from Chapter 21 = Thursday 13 March, and never to be reconciled against the real calendar.**

**Batch 0004 as written, line by line: Saturday 14 March into Monday 16 March · Wednesday 18 March into Friday 20 March · Wednesday 25 March into Friday 27 March · Monday 30 March into Wednesday 1 April · Friday 3 April into Monday 6 April · Wednesday 8 April into Friday 10 April · Saturday 11 April into Monday 13 April · Friday 17 April into Monday 20 April · Friday 24 April into Sunday 26 April · Monday 27 April into Wednesday 29 April 2015. THE BATCH RUNS SATURDAY 14 MARCH TO WEDNESDAY 29 APRIL 2015 AND DOES NOT GO PAST IT.**

**MONDAY 23 MARCH AND WEDNESDAY 15 APRIL 2015 ARE ON THE CALENDAR AND NO CHAPTER IS DATED TO EITHER, AND NOTHING WHATSOEVER HAPPENS ON WEDNESDAY 15 APRIL 2015.** **ONLY THREE CARD WINDOWS CONTAIN A SUNDAY — Chapter 0135 (Sunday 5 April), Chapter 0137 (Sunday 12 April) and Chapter 0139 (Sunday 26 April) — and those are the only three chapters in which the hour between six and seven was staged. The other seven refer to it and do not stage it.**

**THE QUESTION COUNT FOR THE BATCH, AND IT IS RIGHT IN EVERY CHAPTER: JONAS MERCER ASKED EXACTLY ONE QUESTION IN THE WHOLE BATCH, AND IT WAS IN CHAPTER 0133, IT WAS SIX WORDS, IT WAS `And who else has got it`, IT WAS ASKED OF A THIRD PARTY IN A CORRIDOR ON THE WEDNESDAY EVENING OF 25 MARCH, AND A CORRIDOR IS A CORRIDOR AND HE KNEW THAT WHEN HE ASKED IT. HE HAS NOW ASKED NO QUESTION OF ANYBODY SINCE THE TWENTY-FIFTH OF FEBRUARY 2015, and that is a change in him and it is deliberate and it is not going to be remarked on again.**

**ONE LATTICE NOTICE IN THE WHOLE BATCH, IN CHAPTER 0136, on the wall of the vestibule: THE REGISTER OF NARROWINGS IS AN ACCOUNT OF NODES THAT ARE NARROWED / A NODE IS NOT A PERSON / A PERSON MAY BE AFFECTED BY EVERY ENTRY ON THIS BOARD AND IS NAMED ON NONE OF THEM / NO PERSON SIGNS THIS SHEET. IT DEFINES NOTHING, INCLUDING *HOLDER*, AND IT REQUIRES NOTHING OF ANYBODY. THE 1 MARCH LINE ON THE ENAMEL STRIP WAS NOT REISSUED, RESTATED OR QUOTED. THE TWO CONDITIONS ON THE OLD WARD BOARD WERE NOT RECONCILED. THE INITIALS ON THE BACK OF THE ENAMEL STRIP WERE NOT DECIDED IN PROSE.**

## 1. THE SAINT ORRA WEST TERM AFTER CHAPTER 140 — THE WHOLE CHAIN, AND WHO READ IT, AND WHAT WAS NOT WRITTEN DOWN

**THE RULE IS UNCHANGED AND IS THE SINGLE MOST MIS-STATED FACT IN THIS ACCOUNT: A TERM IS SIX DAYS. IT IS READ AT 22:00 ON THE SATURDAY NIGHT BEFORE THE SUNDAY IT STARTS ON. THE GATE OPENS AT 07:00 ON THAT SUNDAY. IT EXPIRES AT 06:00 ON THE FOLLOWING SUNDAY. THE SATURDAY NIGHTS ARE WHAT ARE A WEEK APART, WHICH IS WHY THERE IS AN HOUR ON EVERY SUNDAY MORNING BETWEEN SIX AND SEVEN WHEN NO TERM IS IN FORCE AT ALL. NEVER CALL THE TERM SEVEN DAYS. NEVER CALL IT A WEEK.**

**THE CHAIN, COUNTED, AND THIS IS THE ONLY CORRECT NUMBERING. 24 Jan = 1 · 31 Jan = 2 · 7 Feb = 3 · 14 Feb = 4 · 21 Feb = 5 · 28 Feb = 6 · 7 Mar = 7 · 14 Mar = 8 · 21 Mar = 9 · 28 Mar = 10 · 4 Apr = 11 · 11 Apr = 12 · 18 Apr = 13 · 25 Apr = 14 · 2 May = 15.**

**AND HERE IS A REPAIR AND IT IS BINDING. THE CHAPTER 0135 CARD AS ISSUED CALLED SATURDAY 28 MARCH THE ELEVENTH. IT IS THE TENTH. THE EIGHTH IS 14 MARCH, THE NINTH IS 21 MARCH, THE TENTH IS 28 MARCH, THE ELEVENTH IS 4 APRIL, THE TWELFTH IS 11 APRIL, THE THIRTEENTH IS 18 APRIL, THE FOURTEENTH IS 25 APRIL, THE FIFTEENTH IS 2 MAY. Chapters 0135, 0137 and 0139 are written on the corrected numbering and the corrected numbering is what every later batch is to use.**

- **THE EIGHTH, read 22:00 Saturday 14 March 2015 → began 07:00 Sunday 15 March → expired 06:00 Sunday 22 March. THIS IS THE ONE HANDOVER STAGED IN THE WHOLE BATCH, IN CHAPTER 0131, AND IT IS THE ONLY ONE THAT MAY EVER BE STAGED IN CHAPTERS 141–144.**
- **THE NINTH, read 22:00 Sat 21 March → 07:00 Sun 22 March → 06:00 Sun 29 March.** Reached. Unwritten. Not staged.
- **THE TENTH, read 22:00 Sat 28 March → 07:00 Sun 29 March → 06:00 Sun 5 April.** Reached. Unwritten. Not staged.
- **THE ELEVENTH, read 22:00 Sat 4 April → 07:00 Sun 5 April → 06:00 Sun 12 April.** Reached. Unwritten. Not staged.
- **THE TWELFTH, read 22:00 Sat 11 April → 07:00 Sun 12 April → 06:00 Sun 19 April.** Reached. Unwritten. Referred to in Chapter 0137 by nobody, with no date on it, and not staged.
- **THE THIRTEENTH, read 22:00 Sat 18 April → 07:00 Sun 19 April → 06:00 Sun 26 April.** Reached. Unwritten. Not staged.
- **THE FOURTEENTH, read 22:00 Sat 25 April → 07:00 Sun 26 April → 06:00 Sun 3 MAY 2015.** Reached. Unwritten. **NOT STAGED — Chapter 0139 refers to it in Jonas's interior in two sentences and does not put anybody in a room. THE EIGHTH OF SATURDAY 14 MARCH IS THE ONLY HANDOVER STAGED IN CHAPTERS 131–140.** **THIS IS THE TERM IN FORCE AT THE END OF BATCH 0004, and it was the one referred to in Chapter 0139, and it expires at six o'clock on Sunday the third of May, and the hour between six and seven on Sunday the third of May is the fifteenth hour of that kind and the fifteenth of them is read at ten o'clock on Saturday the second of May, and there is no date on any of that.**

**FOURTEEN SATURDAY NIGHTS HAD BEEN REACHED BY THE END OF CHAPTER 0140 AND NOT ONE OF THEM HAS A DATE WRITTEN DOWN ANYWHERE IN THIS CITY, and there is not going to be one, and the reason is that a person is not required to be told and there is nobody in this borough who is required to write it down.**

**WHO READ THEM ALOUD, on every one of them since 3 January 2015: EZRA MBEKI, in and out of that building since 1992, and TREVOR NUNN, 58, a night porter, nineteen years, who is not on a roster because the roster does not exist. Neither is on any instrument in this city. Neither knew until about nine o'clock that they were going to be there. It took about nine minutes and then they sat down and went home at about half past six. NOTHING WAS WRITTEN DOWN, nothing is going to be, and the reason it is not an oversight is that it is nobody's job.**

**AND NEITHER MBEKI NOR NUNN KNOWS THAT A MAN REFUSED OUT LOUD, IN A CORRIDOR, IN DAYLIGHT, ON 2 MARCH 2015, TO BE IN THAT ROOM, AND NEITHER OF THEM IS GOING TO BE TOLD, AND THERE IS NO WAY IN THIS BOROUGH TO TELL THEM. AND NEITHER OF THEM HAS EVER BEEN ASKED ABOUT ANY OF IT BY ANYBODY AND NEITHER OF THEM EVER WILL BE, AND THE REASON IS NOT DISCRETION. THE REASON IS THAT THERE IS NOTHING TO ASK ABOUT. THE LAST OCCASION ON WHICH ANY PERSON IN THAT BUILDING CAME WITHIN A CORRIDOR OF THE SUBJECT WAS IN THE FIRST WEEK OF APRIL 2015, WHEN A MAN STOOD AT A WALL ON A FRIDAY MORNING, ASKED NOTHING ABOUT A GATE, A ROTA OR A PERSON, AND NOBODY THERE KNEW WHY HE HAD COME AND NOBODY THERE IS GOING TO ASK HIM.**

**THE STATE OF THE GATE ON LEVEL TWO, ON WEDNESDAY 29 APRIL 2015, AND IT IS THE SAME AS IT WAS ON 2 APRIL 2014 AND NOTHING IN THIS BATCH TOUCHED IT: the rota has still not been rebuilt and cannot be; the 11:00–13:30 row is still empty, still not shaded, and still does not say *vacant*; Bram Osei's Thursday is still refused in his own handwriting with *no reason I can give* under a *no reason given* in somebody else's hand, he has asked twice that the second line not be looked at, and nobody looked at it in this batch and nobody is going to; Priya Ranganathan's card is still in a plastic sleeve on the Friday and Saturday rows because she put it there. THE ELEVEN-ROW CLIPBOARD WITH THE FIRST FOUR ROWS WRITTEN ON IN OCTOBER BY THE MAN WHO THEN STOPPED IS STILL ON THE FOURTH CHAIR AND WAS NOT TOUCHED.**

**AND THE BRAM OSEI MOVEMENT, WHICH IS THE ENGINE OF CHAPTERS 0135 AND 0139 AND IS NOT RESOLVED. On Friday 3 April 2015, in a ground-floor corridor at Saint Orra west, in daylight, and without having been asked anything, BRAM OSEI SAID: `You have not been down.` It was said twenty-seven days after the seventh of March and twenty days after the fourteenth. It is true. It is in one porter's ear and it is worth nothing. It was said twenty-seven days after the seventh of March and twenty days after the fourteenth. AND A PORTER IS A PERSON WHO CAN TELL THE DIFFERENCE BETWEEN A MAN WHO IS THERE AND A MAN WHO IS NOT, and that is the first time anybody has established that in this account, and it was established by a man noticing on his own. JONAS MERCER COULD NOT STOP IT AND WENT THROUGH THREE WAYS OF TRYING IN ABOUT NINE MINUTES AND ALL THREE WERE CLOSED: he could not ask a porter not to say it, because he had told one on 2 March that he would not ask him a question and a request is a question with better manners; he could not explain it, because a paragraph in a corridor is a form and it is the first of a series and the man giving it would be the man who wrote it; and he could not be there, because a fourth time in a corridor is a rota with one name in it. HE DID NOT STOP IT. THE COST WAS THAT A CORRECT THING WAS GOING TO HAPPEN IN A BUILDING AND HE WAS THE ONLY PERSON HE KNEW OF WHO COULD SEE IT HAPPENING, and nobody was harmed and nobody was helped.**

## 2. WHAT THE DRIFT LOOKS LIKE FROM THE INSIDE, AND THAT NOBODY CAME BACK TO ARGUE A STATE

**THE ENGINE OF BATCH 0004 AND ITS FINDING, STATED ONCE. A STATE GIVEN ON A DAY IS A STATE ABOUT THE PERSON WHO GAVE IT. There is nobody in this city who can require a records supervisor to say what a thing is, so the only thing anybody can be handed is what a man saw from where he was standing at ten past ten on a Tuesday, and that is a true sentence, and it is in a hand, and it has a date on it, and it is wrong.**

**THE FIRST VISIBLE ARTEFACT OF THE DRIFT, STAGED IN CHAPTER 0131, MONDAY 16 MARCH 2015, AND IT COST NOBODY ANYTHING.** A man of about sixty, a carter with a galvanised can he puts nothing into and has never been in that vestibule in his life, asked what was in the trough by the lock-ups ON THE SECOND OF FEBRUARY. **She answered him in about eleven seconds, in three halves, and gave the state, and the state was right about the second of February and wrong about the trough, and the wrongness is not a fault of the counter. It is a fact about a Tuesday. The man standing in front of her had a bowser to fill and a lunchtime to decide by and told her what he could see from where he was standing, and she wrote it down because he asked her what was true on a day, and she is not required to make a state and nobody can make her. The green in the bottom of that trough was what a market has been putting in a trough for forty years, settling out of water that came off a roof, and what the man put in himself was not what made it green, and that was not said to him as a fact, because a records counter with an opinion about a trough is a service. He went out with the can and did not put the drum in. Nobody was hurt. Nobody was helped. The register did not move.**

**AND THE SECOND KIND OF DRIFT, WHICH IS WORSE, AND IT IS JONAS'S FINDING OF CHAPTER 0131, IN FOUR LINES IN A PAD IN A SHOP: a state that has gone stale belongs to a THING, and a thing is out there and anybody may look at it, and that is the closest this borough gets to a correction and it has no form in it. A state that has been contradicted belongs to a PERSON, and it cannot be looked at, because the only person who can contradict it is the man who said it, and he has not come back, and nobody can ask him to, and a person may not be required to answer anything including a correction. ONE HAS AN OWNER AND THE OTHER HAS A PERSON, AND THIS BOROUGH HAS NO INSTRUMENT FOR EITHER. The first kind rots. The second kind cannot be moved at all, and the second kind is the one in the drawer under that counter.**

**AND NOBODY CAME BACK TO ARGUE A STATE, WHICH IS THE WHOLE OF IT. ABU BAKAYOKO'S FIRST SLIP, FIVE NODES ON ONE SLIP, DATED 26 JANUARY 2015, IS SEVEN WEEKS OLD AND IS IN AN INSIDE POCKET AND HAS NOT BEEN LOOKED AT SINCE THE DAY HE WROTE IT DOWN IN FRONT OF HER. He came on 2 February and on 13 February and has not come since, and it is a Friday, and if he comes on a Friday for another four years that is not a man, that is a rota with one name on it that nobody wrote down and that nobody could write down. HE IS THE ONLY PERSON WHO COULD CORRECT ANYTHING HE WAS TOLD AND HE IS NOT COMING BACK AND THERE IS NO REASON HE WOULD.**

**AND CHAPTER 0138 IS THE INSIDE OF IT, AND IT IS THE FINDING OF THE MONTH. A STATE GIVEN ON A DAY STOPS BEING A STATE ABOUT A DAY AND BECOMES AN OBJECT IN A COAT. It then needs a procedure, and there is no procedure on the sheet, because there is no name box and no number and no box at all, and a document with no procedure cannot be paid, cannot be returned, cannot be filed and cannot be destroyed. So it goes in a pocket. And nobody comes back to argue it, because a man with a piece of paper in a coat has no way of knowing whether he has been told something true, and the only way to know is to go back and ask, and going back is a decision, and nobody is required to make a decision. **That is the drift, and it is not a thing the counter does. It is a thing that happens to a person who is handed a correct answer and given nothing to do with it.**

## 3. THE SHELF, AND WHY NINE AND ELEVEN MAY NEVER BE ADDED

**NINE PIECES OF PAPER WERE IN THE DRAWER UNDER THE GROUND-FLOOR COUNTER AS AT 9 MARCH 2015 AND THERE ARE MORE NOW AND NOBODY CAN SAY HOW MANY, WHICH IS CORRECT. IN CHAPTER 0138 JONAS MERCER WROTE THAT THE NUMBER IS NOT NINE ANY MORE AND THAT HE HAS NOT COUNTED IT AND IS NOT GOING TO AND SHE IS NOT GOING TO TELL HIM. NOBODY, INCLUDING THE NARRATION AND INCLUDING JONAS MERCER, STATES A COUNT OF THE DRAWER AFTER 9 MARCH 2015, AND IN CHAPTER 0136 SHE IS ASKED FOR A DAY AND REFUSES WITHOUT A FIGURE, AND IN CHAPTER 0131 SHE DECLINES TO ASK FOR A DAY WITHOUT ONE, AND IN NEITHER CASE DOES ANYBODY PUT A NUMBER IN HER MOUTH. THIS IS THE BATCH'S HARDEST RULE AND IT HOLDS.**

**THE NINE, EXACTLY, AS AT 9 MARCH 2015, UNCHANGED: 26 January, Abu Bakayoko, five nodes on one slip · 2 February, Abu, the trough · 2 February, Emmanuel Otieno, the tap on the yard wall · 4 February, Otieno · 5 February, Howard Bramble, the standpipe at the bottom of his car park · 5 February, Otieno · 6 February, Otieno · 13 February, Abu, five things with the sixth declined · and one written on a Friday afternoon in the last week of February to somebody who was not in a room with anybody, about whom nobody has said a word and nobody is going to.**

**A TENTH SLIP WAS WRITTEN ON MONDAY 16 MARCH 2015 AND IT IS ABOUT THE TROUGH BY THE LOCK-UPS, it went into the inside of a coat, and on 20 April the man who is carrying it asked whether it was a receipt, and it is not a receipt, and it cannot be paid, cannot be returned, cannot be filed and cannot be thrown away, and he put it back in the inside of his coat, where it had been, and that was the end of it, and nobody helped him and nobody was harmed. HE IS NOT ON ANY LIST.**

**NINA Calder gives no count to anybody. Nobody in this batch asked her for one and Jonas Mercer did not ask her and did not count the drawer and did not look. THE NUMBER IS THE COUNT OF THIS ACCOUNT AND NOT THE COUNT OF A PERSON AND NO CHARACTER KNOWS IT.**

**NINE SLIPS IS A COUNT OF DOCUMENTS. ABOUT ELEVEN PEOPLE A DAY IS A COUNT OF PEOPLE. THEY ARE TWO NUMBERS ABOUT TWO DIFFERENT THINGS AND THEY MAY NEVER BE ADDED TOGETHER, and there is still no form in this borough, on any counter, on any tray, in any office, on which anybody could write that they are not a series, and a box that says *not a series* is a series. The reason is hers and was given out loud on 2 February: a slip is only written when a person stands in front of her and asks what was true on a day, and a form that added the two together would be a form that said *this counter sees eleven people and produces one document*, and that is a level of service, and there is no level of service in that room. THE THROUGHPUT IS ABOUT ELEVEN PEOPLE A DAY AND WENT UP FROM ABOUT NINE ON THE TWENTY-SIXTH OF JANUARY, AND IT IS ON THE BACK OF A HAND AND IN NO BOOK. IN CHAPTER 0132 HE COUNTED THE PEOPLE THROUGH THE DOOR ONCE, BETWEEN ABOUT TEN PAST THREE AND ABOUT HALF PAST THREE, AND HE COUNTED FIVE, WHICH IS NOT A DAILY FIGURE, AND HE STOPPED, AND THE REASON HE STOPPED IS A FINDING AND NOT A VICTORY: a count of people through a door is a level of service the moment anybody can be held to it, and the day a man can be held to it there is a morning somebody can say *we can only do eight today* and be entirely correct, and he came within about nine seconds of being the man who started.**

**AND THE THING THE PEOPLE WHO COME IN ARE NOT: SOMEBODY WHO ASKS A QUESTION ABOUT A DAY HAS ALREADY DECIDED THAT A DATE IS WORTH HAVING, AND THAT DECISION TAKES SOMEBODY ABOUT FOUR MONTHS AND A TROUGH, AND IT IS NOT SOMETHING A COUNTER CAN HAND OUT, AND IF THE COUNTER HANDED IT OUT IT WOULD BE A SERVICE AND IT WOULD SCALE AND IT WOULD BE A THING YOU COULD BUY. SOMEBODY WHO DOES NOT ASK IS NOT REFUSING ANYTHING. HE HAS A CAN, OR A DRUM, OR A BAG, OR A ROUTE, AND HE IS IN A HURRY. A CONTRADICTION REQUIRES A SECOND VISIT. A SECOND VISIT IS A DECISION. NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH IS REQUIRED TO DECIDE ANYTHING AT ALL. THE DRIFT IS INVISIBLE FROM INSIDE THAT ROOM AND IT IS INVISIBLE FOR A REASON NOBODY WANTS TO HEAR, AND THE REASON IS THAT THE ONLY PEOPLE WHO COULD CONTRADICT A STATE ARE THE PEOPLE WHO HAVE ALREADY ASKED ONCE.**

**AND THE FUTURE, STATED OUT LOUD TWICE IN CHAPTERS 0136 AND 0138 AND IT IS THE THING THE BATCH IS BUILDING TOWARDS: A REGISTER THAT GROWS WHEN NOBODY IS HURT IS A REGISTER SOMEBODY CAN HOLD OVER YOU. A DRAWER OF SLIPS GROWS EXACTLY THE SAME WAY. OUT OF CORRECT THINGS DONE BY CAREFUL PEOPLE. NOBODY IS HURT BY IT. IT GROWS AT THE RATE OF A PERSON WALKING THROUGH A DOOR AND ASKING A QUESTION THAT SOMEBODY IS NOT REQUIRED TO ASK. **THE DIFFERENCE BETWEEN THE REGISTER AND THE DRAWER IS ONE LINE LONG: NOBODY EVER WROTE A RULE FOR THE PERSON.** The register has nineteen entries and a blank line under the nineteenth and no rule about who may write under the nineteenth. The drawer has no line. Not a blank one. A PERSON HAS NO LINE AT ALL. And the ninth seat is not told any of this and is not going to be.

**AND TWO DRAWERS IN TWO VEHICLES THAT BELONG TO NOBODY WHO IS KEEPING THEM, both growing, and in about four years somebody in this city is going to be able to say how many, and how many is a capacity, and a capacity is a promise, and the cutting will not be done by anybody cruel. It will be done by a number. Nobody has to cut anything. The number does it by existing.**

**AND THE HOOK, AND IT IS NOW A STATED FINDING AND NOT A HOOK: A SLIP IN A DRAWER IS GOING TO BE EVIDENCE ONE DAY. NOT HIS EVIDENCE. A PIECE OF PAPER THAT SOMEBODY KEPT BECAUSE THEY COULD NOT THINK WHAT ELSE TO DO WITH IT, HANDED OVER BY A PERSON WHO THINKS THEY ARE HELPING. And there is no version of that piece of paper in which the person it was written for is in it, because they were not, because they are never in it, because there is no name box and there is not going to be one. And nobody is going to be able to say afterwards whether it was a service or a choice, and neither is he.**

## 4. FINDABILITY, AND THE REFUSAL TO USE HIS OWN METHOD, AND THE MAN AT THE BOARD OF FORTY SLOTS

**THE MAN WHO TURNS THE BUCKET AT THE BOARD OF FORTY SLOTS IS ONE OF THE SIX LOWLAND WATER POINTS. It is a stand with a bucket on a chain and a board of forty slots at a bus turning head, and it works because a man puts money in the slot and takes the bucket and washes it and puts it back, and the man who runs it could not tell you his name either, and that sentence was said out loud in a room in daylight **on Tuesday the thirteenth of January 2015** by Abu Bakayoko, fifty-eight, and it is still the whole of what anybody knows. **The fourteenth of January is not a date anything in this account happens on, and the sentence is his and not Jonas Mercer's.** HE IS NOT FINDABLE FROM ANY RECORD IN THIS CITY AND HAS NEVER BEEN, and there is no party, no reference, no holder field, no name box, no return, no rota, no minute and no form on which his name could be written, and the reason there is no record of him is not that he was missed. It is that nothing in this borough has ever had to write down a man who turns a bucket.**

**AND THE STAND IS IN A MINUTE. The six lowland water points are in paragraph 6.1 of the minute of 19 May at Tier 1, £11,280 a year, recorded as received and not as evidence, and a green pillar with a bucket on a chain and a board of forty slots is a shape in a paragraph with a number in it, and a man in a corridor in a haversack in about nine seconds could go from that paragraph to a bus turning head and stand there, and there would be a man in front of him every day for as long as the pillar stands. NOBODY WOULD HAVE TO SPEND ELEVEN SECONDS. THE PRICE ALREADY DID IT.**

**AND THE FINDING OF CHAPTER 0132, AND IT RECASTS THE THIRTEENTH OF MARCH WITHOUT RESTATING IT: FINDABILITY IS NOT A THING THIS BOROUGH HAS BEEN MINTING. FINDABILITY IS WHAT A PRICE DOES WHEN THERE IS NO COUNTER IN FRONT OF IT. A COUNTER IS THE CHEAPEST PRIVACY DEVICE ANYBODY IN THIS CITY HAS EVER BUILT AND IT COST NOTHING AND NOBODY DESIGNED IT AND THERE IS NO FORM FOR IT: there are three feet of wood between a person and a person, and a man can come in off the street with a payment or a complaint and be answered by somebody he cannot see past, and nothing about him is required to go anywhere. THE MAN AT THE BOARD OF FORTY SLOTS HAS NO WALL, BECAUSE HE HAS TO TAKE THE MONEY, AND HE IS THE MOST FINDABLE MAN IN THE LOWLAND AND HAS BEEN FOR TWENTY-SIX YEARS AND NOBODY DID IT.**

**AND THE FIRST TIME IN NINE MONTHS THAT HE HAS REFUSED TO USE HIS OWN METHOD, STATED OUT LOUD IN A SHOP ON FRIDAY 20 MARCH 2015, GIVEN TO BEATRICE OSUNDE, WHO DID NOT ARGUE WITH IT AND DID NOT IMPROVE IT: HE DID NOT GO TO THE BUS TURNING HEAD. Three ways of going, and every one of them is a service: (1) ask the man his name, which makes a man findable; (2) describe the stand to somebody, which puts a shape in a corridor; (3) stand there, which makes the finder into the handle. **THE FINDER IS THE HANDLE. THE CHEAPEST HANDLE IN THIS CITY IS NOT A DATE. IT IS A PERSON WALKING UP TO A PILLAR AND BEING HANDED A BUCKET. AND THE MOST EXPENSIVE THING ANYBODY CAN DO TO A MAN WHO ALREADY HAS ONE IS TO GO AND INTRODUCE HIMSELF.** A priced-access case is not a recognition case. Being seen because you charge is not being recognised, and a record that put him in it as a person would be a record of a price, and if the price came down the record would be wrong and nobody could tell. HE DID NOT GO ON THE WEDNESDAY, THE THURSDAY OR THE FRIDAY, and nothing in that week said a man had chosen anything. THE STAND WENT ON WORKING, THE WATER WENT ON COSTING WHAT IT COSTS, THE MAN WENT ON BEING FINDABLE, NOBODY WAS HELPED, NOBODY WAS HURT, AND THE SIXTH OF THE SIX IS STILL NOT NAMED IN ANY DOCUMENT THAT LEAVES THE STREET.**

**AND ON FRIDAY 20 APRIL 2015 THE PRICE OF FINDABILITY CAME BACK AT HIM FOR THE FIRST TIME, IN HIS OWN PERSON, AND IT IS THE SECOND MOUTH OF THE THIRTEENTH OF MARCH. A woman of about thirty came into that vestibule and asked NINA CALDER, in front of him, at the wall, four feet away: `Is the man who stands at the wall on a list.` She was told no, in about four seconds, and she was told the second half as well, which is that he is right and it does not help her, and there is no form on any counter in this building that would take her question, and if there were one then in about four years there would be a list of people who stand at walls. She said `That's a shame` and went out into the street and did not come back. **THE COUNTER WAS RIGHT. A COUNTER THAT ANSWERS ABOUT A PERSON IS A SERVICE. JONAS MERCER STOOD AT THAT WALL FOR ABOUT NINE MINUTES AND SAID NOTHING, AND THERE WAS NO POINT AT WHICH HE COULD HAVE SAID ANYTHING, AND THERE IS NO POINT AT WHICH ANYBODY COULD. The price of the floor is being paid in a currency where the only thing anybody can do about it is refuse, and refusing does not make the person safer, it makes them unattacked. He has been living on the difference between those two things for four months and it is the only instrument in this borough that works and IT DOES NOT PROTECT ANYBODY. IT ONLY MEANS NOBODY IS AIMING.**

## 5. WHAT A METHOD DID ON A SUNDAY, AND WHERE IT WENT, AND THAT IT TRAVELS SIDEWAYS

**EMMANUEL OTIENO, 38, IS PERMITTED A CLOSE POV IN CHAPTER 0133 AND WAS GIVEN ONE. Wednesday 25 March 2015, about two in the afternoon, a kitchen on the top floor of a building on a road he has never seen the name of on any paper, one window, a yard, a table with two chairs, a plate in the sink with the pattern worn through in the middle, and a daughter of nine at school until half past three who comes in on the left foot first and puts her shoes on wrong, and if the shoes are not by the door she does them on the pavement. He put them by the door.**

**AND HERE IS WHAT HE DID WITH A THING HE WORKED OUT ALONE, AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH TAUGHT HIM IT AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH IS ABLE TO TELL HIM WHAT TO DO WITH IT. He gave it away. Three times. And he wrote nothing down and told nobody and there is no form in this borough on which anybody could record that a man of thirty-eight taught three people a thing he worked out himself.**

1. **A woman of about thirty-one at the depot**, four years, up two nights running with a mother in a block of flats, who asked him on a Tuesday in February how he knew a thing was true on a day, and he told her, and she wrote nothing down either, and he was prouder of that than of anything he has done.
2. **A man of about fifty-five on the second floor of that building**, who does not work shifts and is on no list anybody has ever seen, who asked him in that kitchen on a Sunday in the middle of March why he was so calm about a landlord, and asked him to say it again in a different way, and he did, twice.
3. **A man about thirty he had met once, in a queue outside a hospital, in the middle of February, over a broken coffee machine, who had been at the same school in a different decade, and who had a daughter too, and who had written a date on the back of his hand and asked him whether that counted. It counted. That was the first time anybody had asked him that. He did not tell him the half about a rent book and is not going to.**

**AND HE HAS A PLASTIC WALLET IN THE GLOVEBOX OF A VAN HE DOES NOT OWN, IN A YARD TWO STREETS AWAY, AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH CAN SAY HOW MANY PIECES OF PAPER ARE IN IT, AND THAT IS CORRECT, AND THE REASON IT IS CORRECT IS THAT HE IS NOT FINDABLE FROM ANY RECORD IN THIS CITY AND HAS NOT ASKED ANYBODY ANYTHING SINCE THE SIXTH OF FEBRUARY. He did not open the glovebox on the Wednesday afternoon, and it is not a thing he is going to start doing, and the reason is that a man who opens a glovebox on a Wednesday afternoon is a man who is looking at it, and a man who is looking at it will open it again.**

**AND HE HAD ALREADY WORKED OUT THE FAR END OF IT, ALONE, IN A KITCHIN, WITH THE TAP RUNNING, IN FEBRUARY, AND TOLD NOBODY. IN ABOUT FOUR YEARS SOMEBODY IS GOING TO WRITE A FORM. The form will be a good form, with a box on it and a reference and a date, and it will say what that counter does, and the woman who hands it over will not have chosen to be in it and will not be able to be un-in it, because a form with a name on it is a duty and a duty cannot be refused after it is printed. AND THE WALL IS NOT THE COUNTER. THE WALL IS THE DAY SOMEBODY CAN SAY *WE CAN ONLY DO EIGHT TODAY* AND BE ENTIRELY CORRECT, and the day that can be said is the day the room is a room with a service in it, and it will not be the man who said it. It will never be the man who said it. It will be the first person who is tired and honest.**

**AND IT TRAVELS SIDEWAYS, WHICH IS THE FINDING OF CHAPTER 0133, AND IT IS THE FIRST TIME IN NINE MONTHS THAT ANYTHING HAS COME OUT OF THAT COUNTER AND TOUCHED A DISTRICT. It did not go upwards — not to a manager, not to a committee, not to a records office, not to a board of nine seats with two chairs empty. It went to a woman on a shift, and a man on a second floor, and a man in a queue, and from a woman on a shift to a girl on a till, and from a girl on a till to a kitchen on the other side of this borough, and every step of that is a person telling a person at a kettle or in a queue or in a doorway, and not one of the steps is a document, and not one of the steps could be produced by anybody, and there is no instrument in this city that can see any of it.**

**AND WHAT A MAN OF THIRTY-FOUR IN A CANTEEN SAID AGAINST A WALL AT ABOUT HALF PAST FIVE ON WEDNESDAY 25 MARCH, AND IT IS THE WHOLE OF THE BATCH: `It's not strange any more, that's all I wanted. There's a girl on our till who's had two of them off a landlord and she's not even surprised, she just said oh, they all do that now.`**

**AND WHY THE FEAR WAS THE WRONG FEAR. Jonas Mercer had been afraid of a form since 4 September and he was right to be and it is not going to happen. WHAT HAS HAPPENED INSTEAD IS THAT IT STOPPED BEING A THING ANYBODY IS DOING. A DISCOVERY HAS BECOME WEATHER. The way you protect a thing like that from being written down is to make sure nobody can describe it, and a man of thirty-eight made sure it could be described, on purpose, out loud, three times, to three people, and nobody in this borough taught him that and nobody in this borough can tell him what to do with it.**

**AND JONAS MERCER ASKED HIS ONE QUESTION OF THE BATCH AND IT WAS SIX WORDS AND IT WAS IN A CORRIDOR AND A CORRIDOR IS A CORRIDOR AND HE KNEW THAT WHEN HE ASKED IT: `And who else has got it.` Three that the man knew of, and more than that by now, and no form for it, and nobody is going to put it on paper. AND HE IS NOT GOING TO GO AND ASK HIM, AND THE REASON IS EIGHT WORDS AND IT GOES IN A PAD AND NOWHERE ELSE: **I AM THE PERSON WHO MADE IT CHEAP. The first time I ask him a question about it, he is a person somebody can ask about, and I have spent nine months refusing to make doors out of people, and I am not going to make one out of the only man in this borough who worked something out on his own and gave it away for nothing.**

**AND ONE OF THE THREE WORKS SOMEWHERE WITH A NAME. The man in the corridor said a name on the Wednesday evening, not slowly and not twice, and Jonas Mercer did not ask him to say it again, and it is a name that is on the drawing layer in this city with a filled square beside it and an expiry and a node number, because eight of the nine shapes on that layer have names and a filled square and the ninth has not got one and never will. **HE DOES NOT KNOW WHICH ONE OF THE THREE IT IS AND HE IS NOT GOING TO FIND OUT, and there is no form in this borough on which he could ask, and asking is how a discovery becomes a finding, and a finding goes in a report, and a report is a thing a person can be held to.** NOBODY WAS HELPED, NOBODY WAS HARMED, NOTHING WAS RESOLVED, AND NOTHING WAS WRITTEN DOWN.**

## 6. THE A WOMAN OF THIRTY-FOUR, TWICE ASKED, AND DID NOT COME, AND THE FOUR LINES

**THE ATTRIBUTION IS NOW FIXED AND IS BINDING, BECAUSE TWO EARLIER DOCUMENTS GOT IT WRONG. JONAS MERCER DID NOT WRITE THE FOUR LINES AND NEVER DID AND HAS NEVER CLAIMED THEM.**

- **WHAT HE DID WAS SAY A NAME OUT LOUD, on Wednesday the third of December 2014, at about twenty to seven in the evening, in a room with about eleven people in it, in about nine seconds.** The minute of that evening says that a name was stated. It does not contain the name. It cannot be produced, argued with, minuted, published, attached to a claim, or found by anybody who was not one of the about eleven people in that room, and there is no list of who was in that room and there is not going to be one.
- **THE FOUR LINES AT THE TOP OF THAT SHEET OF A4 ARE THE MINUTE-TAKER'S. They are in biro. They were written on Wednesday the twenty-first of January 2015 in about four minutes, on a second sheet that had nothing at the top of it when she took it. Underneath them, in a different hand, are four lines that are not hers and are nobody else's, and nobody has said out loud in a room that those four lines came from the woman, and nobody in this borough is going to say it.**
- **NO CHAPTER MAY PUT HIS HAND TO THE PAPER OR THE PEN, AND HE HAS NOT AND WILL NOT.** On 24 December, on a bench in a shop, Beatrice Osunde told him the rule and he has kept it every day since: *You have been writing things down since April for the people in the room. That last one is yours. Do not lose it and do not show it to anybody, and there is no box on it anywhere and that is also the point.*
- **The other piece of paper is a letter, in a handbag, in a kitchen off a landing. Nobody in this borough has been in that kitchen, and it has not been opened, and it is not going to be, and it is not a document.**

**AND THERE IS NO NUMBER IN THIS BOROUGH FOR ANYBODY TO TELEPHONE, AND IT IS NOT ON A LIST, AND IT IS NOT GOING ON ONE, AND THE REASON IS FOUR WORDS LONG:**

> **NOBODY OWNS THAT NUMBER.**

**A LIST OF TELEPHONE NUMBERS IS A LIST OF PEOPLE. A LIST OF PEOPLE WHO CANNOT BE REACHED IS A LIST OF PEOPLE WHO ARE NOT IN A ROOM. A BODY OF NINE SEATS CANNOT REQUIRE A PERSON TO DO ANYTHING, NOT A FORM, NOT A SIGNATURE, NOT A TELEPHONE CALL, AND NOT TO ATTEND A SITTING IT IS NOT REQUIRED TO HOLD, and the seven people in those seats have not met since the middle of January and are not going to meet in April. NOBODY CAN MAKE HER COME, AND NOBODY CAN EVEN MAKE ANYBODY TRY TO MAKE HER COME, because a body cannot require a person to do anything, including the thing that would require the person to be required to do anything first, and that is not a paradox anybody in this borough has solved and it is not going to be solved.**

**AND NOTHING HAPPENED. Four months, two rooms, in daylight, with papers. The printed extract from Committee Services went back into a tray in a hall in Tideglass on Monday 30 March at about eleven o'clock, and the putting back took a woman about four seconds, and the putting back is the only thing anybody in this borough has done about that person in four months. HER NAME IS NOT ON IT. THERE IS NO NAME BOX ON IT. IT HAS NEVER HAD HER NAME ON IT AND IT IS NOT GOING TO BE PUT ON IT, AND THERE IS NO INSTRUMENT IN THIS CITY BY WHICH ANYBODY COULD PUT A NAME ON A PIECE OF PAPER THAT HAS NO NAME BOX.**

**AND THE FINDING, AND IT IS THE ONLY HONEST THING ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH HAS PRODUCED ABOUT A PERSON IN NINE MONTHS: A HOLE IN THE RECORD IS THE ONLY HONEST THING. Everything else this borough has produced in nine months is a thing — a sentence, a state, a date, a register with a blank line under it, a form with no name box, a chair that does not exist, a term read aloud in a room with four chairs in it. This is the only one that is an absence, and the absence is a person, and it is the only one nobody can use.**

**AND THE PRICE, WHICH IS EXACT, AND NOBODY HAD WORKED IT OUT BEFORE: THE MOST EXPENSIVE THING ANYBODY IN THIS CITY HAS PRODUCED ABOUT A PERSON IN NINE MONTHS IS A PIECE OF A4 IN A DRAWER WITH NO KEY ON IT, AND NOBODY IS GOING TO OPEN IT, AND THE PRICE OF THAT PIECE OF PAPER IS THAT A WOMAN OF THIRTY-FOUR IS A PERSON THIS BOROUGH CAN SAY NOTHING ABOUT AT ALL. Not nothing nearly. Nothing at all. No claim, no reference, no return, no field, no counter, no list, no form, no minute, no slip, no extract, no envelope with a departmental reference, and no telephone number, and there is no route to her that is not a document, and every one of the five documents that exist is a document that cannot be produced by anybody she has not spoken to. **AND THAT IS THE CORRECT OUTCOME, IT IS NOT A CONSOLATION, AND THERE IS NO ARGUMENT IN THIS BOROUGH IN WHICH IT COULD BE MADE BETTER BY ANYBODY DOING MORE.**

**AND THE ONE DRAWNER WITH NO KEY IS THE ONLY HONEST THING ABOUT HER, WHICH IS TWELVE WORDS AND IS NOT A PROMISE AND IS NOT ON A PAD.**

## 7. A REASON, GIVEN TO SOMEBODY WITH A PEN, AND WHO KEEPS THE MINUTES

**THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THE MINUTES IS THE SAME PERSON WHO STANDS AT THAT COUNTER, AND THAT IS NOW CANON, AND IT WAS ESTABLISHED IN CHAPTER 0136 AND JONAS MERCER TOOK TEN WEEKS TO WORK IT OUT BECAUSE HE HAD BEEN ASSUMING THERE WAS A BOX SOMEWHERE THAT SAID SO.**

- **There is no box.** There is a sheet that says *minute taken by* and there is a name in it and that is the only name-box on the whole of the ground floor. **The sheet is the minute until about four o'clock and after that it is only a sheet**, and she is the only person in this city who can end a minute by picking one up and putting it down.
- **If there were a box that tied *minute taken by* to *counter, ground floor*, that would be a rota with one name in it, and the name would be hers, and the rota would be the establishment of a service floor in a building, by a person, in about eleven seconds, and it would be the only thing anybody in this borough has actually wanted since the fourth of September.**
- **She is not on the board and not on a rota and is not going to be on either. She is about fifty, has nineteen years in that office, designing lists since 1999, and comes down at about ten and about three because nobody has ever told her to and nobody could.**

**AND ON WEDNESDAY 8 APRIL 2015, IN DAYLIGHT, IN A ROOM WITH ABOUT ELEVEN PEOPLE IN IT, SHE SAID A SENTENCE ABOUT HERSELF. NOBODY ASKED HER. IT TOOK ABOUT ELEVEN SECONDS. IT IS NOT A REASON AGAINST A REGISTER ENTRY AND IT IS NOT GOING ON A SLIP.**

> **I DECIDE IT, AND THERE IS NOBODY I AM ALLOWED TO ASK WHETHER I HAVE DECIDED IT RIGHT, AND THAT IS THE WHOLE OF IT, AND I HAVE HAD THAT FOR NINETEEN YEARS.**

**AND WHAT SHE HAD DONE, AND IT IS THE FINDING OF CHAPTER 0136: SHE DID NOT SAY IT TO HIM. SHE SAID IT TO THE ROOM. A SENTENCE SAID TO ONE PERSON IN A CORRIDOR IS A THING TWO PEOPLE KNOW AND IT CAN BE USED AND IT BELONGS TO THE TWO OF THEM — a porter said that to a man with a trolley on 2 March and he was exactly right, and a man of thirty-four in a canteen in March said the other side of it: a thing that is said in a room is a thing a room knows. **THERE IS NO BOX ON A ROOM.** No form in this borough has a room on it, no return, no field, no reference, no index and no rota, and a vestibule eleven feet by nine full of people on a Wednesday morning in April is not an establishment and cannot be interrogated. She chose the room because a room cannot be asked what happened in it and a corridor can, and a corridor is where a man asks a porter a question. A person who has been refusing things for nineteen years has had nobody ask her for a reason, and a person who has never been asked for a reason does not know whether her reasons are any good, and the only way to find out whether a sentence is true is to say it out loud where it can be overheard.**

**AND THE OTHER HALF, WHICH IS THE REASON, AND IT IS ABOUT NINE SECONDS: she said it to him and not to the wall because he has had a pen since 4 September and has been writing down why other people say no for nine months, and nobody in this city has ever once asked him for one of his, and she found out in about four minutes on the Wednesday morning that she had got one, and had never told anybody that she did not have one until he handed it to her by not asking.**

**HE WROTE IT DOWN, IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS, ON THE LEFT-HAND PAGE OF A PAD, IN A CORRIDOR, BECAUSE THERE WAS NO MINUTE — THE BOARD WAS NOT SITTING AND IS NOT REQUIRED TO. She saw him do it. She put her pen down, which is the thing she does about four times a year, and said: `Nobody in this borough has ever written down one of mine. You are going to. And it is going in a book that goes in a shop at the end of a street and it is not going anywhere, and I know that, and I am letting it, and do not come back and tell me what you did with it.`**

**AND THE CONSEQUENCES, ALL OF THEM, AND NONE OF THEM MAY BE UNDONE IN CHAPTERS 141–144:**

1. **THAT SENTENCE IS NOW IN ABOUT ELEVEN PEOPLE AND IN NO DOCUMENT, and that is precisely the thing she refused on 2 January, and it has happened anyway, and it was not anybody's fault, and nobody in this borough did it to her, and she went into that room on purpose and had worked out on the way in what she was going to do.**
2. **NOBODY MAY ASK HER A QUESTION ABOUT IT AFTERWARDS AND NOBODY DID. HE DID NOT ASK HER WHETHER IT WAS GOING IN THE MINUTE AND SHE DID NOT OFFER. FOUR PEOPLE IN THAT ROOM HEARD IT AND TWO OF THEM LOOKED AT HER AND ONE OF THEM SAID `what was that` ABOUT FOUR MINUTES LATER TO SOMEBODY ELSE AND WAS TOLD IT WAS NOTHING, and nothing was the correct answer and it was not a lie. There is no item about the eighth of April in any minute and there is not going to be one.**
3. **IT IS NOW IN ONE HAND IN A BOOK THAT GOES NOWHERE, WHICH IS THE FIRST TIME ANYTHING SHE HAS SAID HAS GONE ANYWHERE AT ALL, AND SHE KNEW THAT WHEN SHE LET HIM DO IT, AND SHE LET IT.** Nobody may open that pad and nobody may ask what is in it. Nobody has asked her about it since and nobody is going to.
4. **M. FURREAUX AND HOWARD BRAMBLE DID NOT ENTER THAT CHAPTER AND MAY NOT BE ARGUED WITH AND MAY NOT COME BACK A THIRD TIME.**
5. **AND SHE IS GOING TO BE PROVED RIGHT ABOUT THE REGISTER, AND IT IS GOING TO BE ABOUT THE SHELF AND NOT ABOUT THE BULB, AND SHE DOES NOT KNOW THAT YET, AND JONAS MERCER DOES AND IS NOT GOING TO TELL HER, and the reason he has written down is that if he told her she would be the woman who was warned.**

**AND HER NINE WORDS WERE SET OUT ONCE, IN CHAPTER 0136, WHERE THE CHAPTER IS ABOUT A SENTENCE BEING GIVEN OUT LOUD, AND NO LATER CHAPTER IN THIS VOLUME MAY RESTATE THEM: a number of what a counter does is a level of service, and the day a floor has a level of service somebody can cut it. And the reason column does not exist and will not exist, and there is still no form on which anybody could write that these are not a series.**

## 8. THE HOUR, AND THE FOUR HUNDRED YARDS, AND WHY HE STOPPED

**THE HOUR BETWEEN SIX AND SEVEN EXISTS ON EVERY SUNDAY AND THREE CHAPTERS STAGED IT AND NOBODY MAY STAGE IT AGAIN WITHOUT A LINE HERE SAYING SO: Chapter 0135, Sunday 5 April 2015 · Chapter 0137, Sunday 12 April 2015 · Chapter 0139, Sunday 26 April 2015. The 26th of April was the fourth of those in four weeks, after the 5th, the 12th and the 19th, and there is no date in this city for any of them and nobody wrote any of them down.**

**AND WHAT A HOUR IS, WHEN A MAN STANDS IN ONE AND IT IS THE ONLY HOUR OF THE WEEK IN WHICH THIS BOROUGH REQUIRES NOTHING OF ANYBODY, WHICH IS THE FINDING OF CHAPTER 0137 AND IS FOUR LINES AND IS NOT GOING ON A WALL: IT IS A FLOOR THAT IS NOT LEVEL, AND THERE IS NOBODY IN IT. A term is a promise made by two tired people at ten o'clock on a Saturday night, and a promise that runs six days and then stops, made by two people who are not on anything, on their own time, with nothing written down, IS THE CHEAPEST THING ANYBODY IN THIS CITY HAS EVER PRODUCED. IN THAT HOUR FOUR HUNDRED AND TWENTY WOMEN ARE NOT ON THE FAR SIDE OF A PARAGRAPH NUMBER, BECAUSE THERE IS NO PARAGRAPH AND NO TERM. IN THAT HOUR THE ELEVEN O'CLOCK TO HALF PAST ONE ROW IS NOT EMPTY, BECAUSE THERE IS NO ROTA AND NO BOARD. IN THAT HOUR THE REGISTER HAS NO NINETEENTH ENTRY WITH A BULB OUT ON IT.**

**AND THE FINDING THAT HE COULD NOT HOLD, WHICH IS THE REAL ONE AND IT IS ABOUT HIM: **THE HOUR CANNOT BE SCHEDULED WITHOUT SOMEBODY BEING REQUIRED TO BE IN IT.** Not a rota. Not a rule. Any schedule at all contains the sentence *somebody will be there*, and a sentence with somebody in it is a requirement, and the registrar put it in a minute in June: a clause that cannot be challenged by a person cannot require them to, and a column with a name in it can be challenged by the person whose name is in it, and a person who is allowed to say no to a column is no longer a column, and then there is nothing on the wall at all. **SO THE ONLY WAY TO KEEP THE HOUR IS TO KEEP IT UNSCHEDULED, AND THE INSTRUMENT THAT KEEPS IT UNSCHEDULED IS THE SAME INSTRUMENT THAT HAS BEEN DOING EVERYTHING ELSE FOR NINE MONTHS, WHICH IS TWO PEOPLE WHO ARE NOT ON ANYTHING TURNING UP AT TEN O'CLOCK ON A SATURDAY BECAUSE NOBODY WROTE ANYTHING DOWN. It is the cheapest thing in this borough. It is the only thing that works. There is no instrument for it and there cannot be one and the attempt to make one is the exact moment this whole thing stops.**

**AND FOUR HUNDRED YARDS IS NOT AN ANSWER EITHER, AND THAT IS THE FINDING OF CHAPTERS 0135, 0137 AND 0139 TOGETHER. The four hundred yards is a piece of wet pavement at a bus turning head, and he has stood on it five times since December. In late December, in the dark. On 3 January 2015, in the rain, at ten o'clock. On 14 March 2015 at ten o'clock, deliberately. On 3 April 2015 at about twenty past six in the morning. On 11 April 2015 at ten past ten at night. **FROM FOUR HUNDRED YARDS YOU CANNOT TELL WHETHER A DOOR IS OPEN OR SHUT, AND THAT IS THE CORRECT AMOUNT OF EVIDENCE.** What it buys is that he cannot be a rota, could not be expected, could not be noticed, could not be seen to be there, could not be asked whether he had been, and could not be a reason that a porter began to look at a corridor. And that stopped being true by the eleventh of April, because four hundred yards had been safe on 3 April only because on 3 April it was a decision he had made on the Friday, and then he stood on the same pavement on the eleventh at about ten past ten at night for about half an hour and worked out that **four hundred yards is a place, and a place a man goes to is the first half of a schedule, and a schedule is a rota with one name in it, and a man who is regularly four hundred yards away is a man who is regularly four hundred yards away.** He stood in the corridor instead, on the Sunday morning at about twenty past six, which was the second time he had ever stood in a corridor on a Sunday morning. **He has stood in one exactly twice, at half past four in the morning on 4 January 2015 and at twenty past six in the morning on 12 April 2015, and the first of those had not been planned for a week and the second one had, and that is the whole of the difference.**

**AND THE MOVE HE HAS NOT MADE AND IS NOT GOING TO MAKE IN CHAPTERS 141–144 WITHOUT A LINE HERE SAYING SO: HE DID NOT OPEN ANYTHING. The claim to open a door in this volume was spent on 28 January 2015 in a room in daylight in about four seconds, and it is the only thing he has ever spent in nine months, and he was not going to go back to a thing he has already spent because it would make the next four days easier. THERE WAS NOTHING IN THAT CORRIDOR HE WAS ALLOWED TO OPEN AND HE OPENED NOTHING AND THERE WAS NO MOMENT IN THE WHOLE OF THE HOUR WHEN HE WANTED TO.**

**AND THE THING THE BATCH ENDS ON, WHICH IS ABOUT HIM AND IS THE FIRST TIME, AND IT IS THE SECOND MOUTH OF EVERYTHING: **HE HAS BEEN DOING IT FOR NOTHING FOR FOUR MONTHS AND THE ENTIRE PRODUCT IS THAT TWO TIRED MEN COULD SAY THAT HE WAS THERE.** That is all. And he has worked out that he would rather be that than a name on a list. **AND THAT IS NOT A PRINCIPLE. IT IS A PRICE.** And there is a difference and he knows exactly what it is, because he has been in a room in daylight on 28 January and has said out loud what a thing like that is worth and it is nothing that anybody can be held to. And Beatrice Osunde told him in December that a thing like that has to be said to somebody four times a day for a week before it holds, **AND HE HAS SAID IT TO HER ONCE, ON MONDAY THE THIRTEENTH OF APRIL 2015, AND HE CAME IN AGAIN ON THE TWENTY-FOURTH WITH THE SAME THING IN HIS MOUTH AND DID NOT SAY IT, and that is the first time in nine months he has come into that shop with a sentence in him and gone out without it, and the reason is not that he has changed his mind. THE REASON IS THAT A THING THAT HAS BEEN SAID ONCE HAS NOT BEEN TESTED, AND A THING THAT HAS NOT BEEN TESTED IS A PREFERENCE. HE IS NOT GOING TO ASK HER AGAIN, HE IS NOT GOING TO ANYBODY ELSE WITH IT, AND NOBODY IS GOING TO BE TOLD. And on the twenty-fourth she said: `The oil came in on Tuesday and the tin is by the door and I would like it moved before the weekend. And that is not the same conversation and I am telling you that it is not the same conversation so that you know I know it is not the same conversation.`**

## 9. WHETHER THE COMPLIANCE PARAGRAPH WAS EVER ANSWERED, AND THE HAND THAT PUTS THE STRIP IN THE DRAWER

**IT WAS NOT ANSWERED. IT WAS NOT ANSWERED BY ANYBODY, EVER, IN THIS BATCH, AND IT IS NOT GOING TO BE ANSWERED, AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH IS ABLE TO ANSWER IT, AND THE BATCH SAYS THAT OUT LOUD AND DOES NOT APOLOGISE FOR IT.**

- **PARAGRAPH 4.11, DECONTAMINATION, WEST SITE, LEVEL ZERO. THE HISTORICAL HALF OF THE WEEKLY VERIFICATION RECORD CANNOT BE RECONSTRUCTED AND THE NON-CONFORMANCE IS CARRIED FORWARD.** There is no name in it because there has never been a name field on the sheet the paragraphs are counted from. Four hundred and twenty women in this borough have instruments out of that room every weekday and every one of them is on the far side of a paragraph number and there is no person on the near side of it.
- **THE HAND THAT PUTS THE STRIP IN THE DRAWER WAS NOT FOUND, WAS NOT ASKED, WAS NOT RUNG, AND HAS NOT BEEN LOOKED FOR.** The machine is still running on four hundred and ten pounds a doctor bought herself after the supplier's contract expired at midnight on 30 April. About four instruments an hour come out of it. **ITS OWN CYCLE RECORD GOES IN A DRAWER IN THE ROOM ABOUT FORTY TIMES A DAY, AND A PIECE OF PAPER DOES NOT PUT ITSELF IN A DRAWER, and that person is not on a form, is not on a rota, is not in a paragraph, and has not been found either, and Jonas Mercer has said out loud in a corridor that he is not going to look for him, and he is not going to.**
- **THE FROST WARNING THAT WAS OUT FOR THE TWENTY-EIGHTH OF FEBRUARY CAME OFF ON THE SECOND OF APRIL 2015. It is eight degrees in that room.**

**AND ROYDEN ACHEBE, 49, WAS NOT TOLD, BY NOBODY, AT ANY POINT IN THIS BATCH.** Jonas Mercer did not go to Gantry Ward, did not telephone, did not use the switchboard, did not go to the team, did not go to the building, did not ask whether he was in, and did not stand on a landing. **What changed in this batch is not Achebe. It is that findability was shown to be something a price does and not something a counter mints, and that is a finding about a man at a bus turning head, and it does not touch the five routes, every one of which is a document and a person who will remember it.** He is forty-nine. He is not untouchable. There is no argument in this borough in which not asking him is the right thing to do. He is not going to be asked.

**AND PETER NAYLOR, 44, A WALL, WAS NOT ASKED ANYTHING, EVER, IN THIS BATCH, AND WAS NOT IN ANY ROOM IN IT AND WAS NOT ON ANY CORRIDOR IN IT, EXCEPT AS A SENTENCE.** He has a fortnight somewhere in this city with a bucket under a tap, and nobody in this borough is able to ask him whether he has got a coat, and nobody helped him, and that is unresolved and stays unresolved.
