11. **A MAN OF FORTY-EIGHT**, in a corridor, in August 2014, about a job in November having a department in it. **UNTOUCHED AND NOT FOUND AND NOT ASKED AND NOT RUNG, and Jonas Mercer has now said out loud, on the record, in a corridor, in daylight, that he is not going to, and the reason is the batch's most exact refusal, and it is the sentence he is going to be judged on.**

**AND TWO OBJECTS WHICH ARE NOT REFUSALS AND ARE NOT ANYBODY'S DUTY AND ARE NOT ADDED TO ANYTHING: A CARD IN A SLOT IN A COUNTER THAT NOBODY EMPTIES, AND A LETTER IN A TRAY THAT NOBODY HAS OPENED IN FRONT OF A SECOND PERSON. Neither has a name on it.**

## 11. THE REGISTER, THE BLANK LINE, THE POST SLOT, AND THE TRAY

**NINETEEN ENTRIES, UNMOVED. IT DID NOT MOVE ONCE IN THIS BATCH. IT DID NOT MOVE ONCE IN BATCH 0003. IT HAS NOT MOVED SINCE WEDNESDAY 26 NOVEMBER 2014. The blank line under the nineteenth has been blank, as at the dates on the page: 110 days on 16 March (Ch 0131) · 114 on 20 March (0132) · 121 on 27 March (0133) · 124 on 30 March (0134) · 133 on 8 April (0136) · 145 on 20 April (0138) · 149 on 24 April and 151 on 26 April (0139) · 154 on 29 April (0140) 2015. Those are the nine figures the chapters actually carry and there is no others. THAT IS ONE HUNDRED AND FIFTY-FOUR DAYS AND IT IS THE LONGEST IT HAS EVER BEEN STILL SINCE THE FOURTH OF AUGUST, AND IT IS THE ONLY FIXED POINT IN THE BUILDING, AND IT IS THE SINGLE MOST LOAD-BEARING OBJECT IN VOLUME 03. Entry 19 is a yard light, the maintenance is a bulb, and the bulb is out.**

**THE SLOT FOR POST: A CARD HAS BEEN IN IT SINCE FRIDAY 20 FEBRUARY 2015. SIXTY-SIX DAYS ON MONDAY 27 APRIL AND SIXTY-EIGHT DAYS ON WEDNESDAY 29 APRIL 2015, AND IT IS THE SECOND FIGURE THAT IS THE FIGURE AT THE END OF THE BATCH.** Four inches by two, folded once, not a form, no crest, no reference, no box, no envelope. **NOBODY HAS TAKEN IT OUT AND NOBODY HAS PUT IT UP AND NINA CALDER HAS NOT READ IT AND IS NOT GOING TO.** A slot in a counter is not hers; a slot that gets emptied is a rule; a rule is a service; a service is a rota with one name on it, and hers is the name it would have. I am not leaving it in it. I am not taking it out of it. Those are different, and the difference is the whole of what this room is. **WHAT IS ON THE CARD IS NOT KNOWN TO ANYBODY IN THIS ACCOUNT AND MAY NOT BE STATED.**

**THE TRAY ON THE SECOND FLOOR: A LETTER WENT IN ON WEDNESDAY 18 FEBRUARY 2015 AT ABOUT HALF PAST NINE, NOT ON BOROUGH STOCK, WITH A DEPARTMENTAL REFERENCE IN THE CORRECT TYPEFACE, ABOUT FOUR LINES LONG. SIXTY-EIGHT DAYS ON MONDAY 27 APRIL AND SEVENTY DAYS ON WEDNESDAY 29 APRIL 2015, AND IT IS THE SECOND FIGURE THAT IS THE FIGURE AT THE END OF THE BATCH.** It is not a reason column. It is the smallest request anybody in this borough has made since the first of March. **NINA CALDER HAS NOT OPENED IT IN FRONT OF A SECOND PERSON, HAS TOLD NOBODY IT IS THERE, HAS NOT PUT IT IN THE TRAY ON THE GROUND FLOOR, AND HAS NOT ANSWERED IT. AND SOMEBODY HAS NOW STARTED TO LEAVE POST ON TOP OF IT, WHICH IS THE OLDEST WAY IN THE WORLD OF SAYING THAT A THING HAS NOT BEEN DEALT WITH.** Jonas Mercer went up to the second floor on 10 April, saw the tray, did not move the post, did not lift the bundle, did not look at the reference, does not know what is under it and is never going to. **AND THE ONE FACT ABOUT THAT DESK THAT IS A FACT AND NOT A STORY, WHICH HE WROTE DOWN: a piece of paper under two other pieces of paper is a piece of paper that has stopped being dealt with, and that is the oldest way in the world of saying so, and anybody who works in an office knows it, and nobody in this borough has to be told it, and there is no instrument anywhere that could go and find a thing like that on a tray and ask what it is. A register with nineteen entries and a blank line under the nineteenth says that nothing was added. A tray with post on top of something says that something was asked for and not dealt with, and it says it in a way that cannot be answered, because answering it would mean taking the post off.**

**WHAT IS IN THE LETTER IS NOT KNOWN TO ANYBODY IN THIS ACCOUNT AND MAY NOT BE STATED IN A ROOM AND MAY NOT BE OPENED IN A DOCUMENT. IT WAS NOT OPENED IN THIS BATCH AND IT IS NOT GOING TO BE OPENED IN CHAPTERS 141–144.**

**AND THE TWO CONDITIONS ARE SIXTEEN INCHES APART AND HAVE NEVER BEEN RECONCILED AND WERE NOT IN THIS BATCH AND MAY NOT BE.** The city's own paper, printed 14 August, with two letters in the corner in a hand that is not a capital hand. And an enamel strip with nothing on its face, undated, unattributed, two letters and one word in pencil on the back of it, and the word is *not mine*. **THE TWO LETTERS ON THE BACK OF THE STRIP ARE NEVER DECIDED IN PROSE.** The 1 March line is not reissued, restated or quoted. **NOTHING DEFINES *HOLDER*.** And the batch's finding about that wall, which is physical and not moral, was not repeated and did not need to be: a person walking under two conditions that do not agree goes faster, because a contradiction is not a pause, it is a floor that is not level, and everybody crosses a floor that is not level faster than a floor that is.

## 12. THE BOARD, THE SEATS, THE NINTH, AND THE THING THAT WAS ASKED IN A ROOM ON 27 APRIL 2015

**THE BOARD STANDS AT SEVEN OF NINE. TWO CHAIRS. THE SEVENTH EMPTY SINCE WEDNESDAY 8 OCTOBER 2014 AND THE EIGHTH SINCE THE EVENING OF WEDNESDAY 26 NOVEMBER 2014. Nobody can fill either and nobody can unfill either and there is no power in this city that could. **THERE IS NO CHAIR OF THAT BODY AND THERE NEVER WAS, and a chair is not one of the three parts of its one power, which are: require a party holding continuity to answer, receive the answer, be asked to consider a matter.** It has not met since the middle of January and is not going to meet in April. Four of the nine have said they will stop if it turns into a job; one of them gave a reason and asked for it not to be written down, and it is not in the minute, and that was agreed and both of those were correct. Nobody has stopped. Nobody has been asked again.**

**AND THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THE MINUTES IS THE SAME PERSON WHO STANDS AT THAT COUNTER, AND THAT WAS ESTABLISHED IN THIS BATCH AND IS NOW CANON — see §7.**

**AND THE NINTH SEAT IS THE ONLY DOOR IN A BUILDING OF THIRTY-ONE RENTED ROOMS, IS FIFTY-FOUR AND UNPAID AND FINDABLE AT A SHOPFRONT SINCE THE FOURTH OF JULY, AND NOBODY HAS TOLD HER ANYTHING AND NOBODY IS GOING TO, AND THE EIGHT DID NOT LEARN WHAT SHE CAN DO, AND NOBODY IN THIS BATCH NOTICED IT, AND IT MAY NOT BE RESOLVED.**

**AND ON MONDAY 27 APRIL 2015, IN A VESTIBULE ELEVEN FEET BY NINE, IN DAYLIGHT, A MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SIX ASKED THE THIRD QUESTION ANYBODY HAS ASKED HIM IN NINE MONTHS, AND JONAS MERCER DECLINED THE QUESTION AS ASKED, PUT ANOTHER IN PLACE OF IT, AND ANSWERED THAT ONE. It is in a public minute with no names in it and nothing in it requires any person to do anything, and the fifth recorded item says: `that this is the third answer given in this room in nine months and that none of the three has been repeated and that none of the three has been placed on a wall.`**

> **IT IS FOR THE PEOPLE THE BOROUGH CANNOT COUNT.**

**THAT IS THE WHOLE OF WHAT A SERVICE FLOOR IS FOR, IN ONE SENTENCE, AND IT IS THE THIRD ANSWER, AND IT IS NEITHER WHAT IT IS MADE OF NOR WHAT IT COSTS. The first was given on 28 January 2015 in five items and about four seconds. The second was given on 13 March 2015 in a sentence and is not to be restated. The third is this. The man of about fifty-six did not ask a fourth question, nobody repeated it, nobody improved it, and the man of about fifty-six still has five words in biro on the back of his left hand that nobody has ever asked him about and nobody is going to.**

**AND WHAT IT IS NOT, AND THE CHAPTER SAYS IT OUT LOUD AND IT IS NOT A RESOLUTION: NAMING A PURPOSE IS NOT PRODUCING ONE. A floor that is for the people the borough cannot count is not a floor that has counted them, and does not seat them, and does not put them in a register, and does not require anybody to do anything, and cannot. THE LOWLAND IS STILL NOT ON THE LAYER, IS STILL NOT IN THE EIGHT DISTRICTS, STILL CANNOT BE SEATED, and the reason is still that a node with no party is not a party and a place is not a person, and that reason is correct and is not going to be fixed. **AND A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED. THAT SENTENCE HAS BEEN TRUE SINCE THE SECOND OF JUNE AND IT HAS NOT BEEN REFUTED AND IT IS NOT REFUTED BY NAMING A PURPOSE, AND SAYING OUT LOUD WHAT A FLOOR IS FOR HAS NOT MADE ONE SINGLE PERSON IN THIS CITY ANY CLOSER TO BEING HELPED, AND EVERYBODY IN THAT ROOM KNOWS IT, AND THAT IS THE CORRECT OUTCOME AND IT IS NOT A VICTORY.**

**AND THE CHOICE OF WHO WAS IN THAT ROOM WAS MADE ON THE PAGE AND NONE OF THEM WAS LEFT OUT BY ANYBODY, AND EVERY ONE OF THEM WAS LEFT OUT ON PURPOSE BY A MAN STANDING AT A WALL: the man of about fifty-six, who is not a member; the woman who keeps the minutes, who is not named in this account and is not going to be; a records supervisor of about fifty who is not on a rota; a clerk who comes down twice a day; a woman of about sixty who has read a board with an empty row on it every morning for eleven months and has never been asked about it; and two people who are on no board. The ninth seat was not there and was not told. The man of forty-four was not there and could not have been asked. The man of forty-nine was not there and could be asked tomorrow and has not been. The man of forty-eight was not there and is not going to be asked. The woman of thirty-four was not there and is not in any room. The woman of seventy-two was not there. The four hundred and twentieth woman was not there and there is no person on the near side of that paragraph. The man of thirty-eight was not there and works nights and one of the three people he taught is somewhere with a name on it and Jonas Mercer does not know which one and is not going to find out and has not written it down.**

## 13. THE STATE OF THE CITY AT THE END OF CHAPTER 140 — Wednesday 29 April 2015, about twenty to eleven in the morning

- **A COUNTER IN A VESTIBULE ELEVEN FEET BY NINE ANSWERS IN ABOUT ELEVEN SECONDS.** A state asked for instead of a list, and a date. It is not a service, it is not on a rota, it does not scale, it cannot be given to anybody, and nobody is required to keep it going. It works because a person is standing at it and that person is not findable and is not on anything.
- **THE REGISTER OF NARROWINGS IS AT NINETEEN AND THE BLANK LINE IS BLANK AND HAS BEEN BLANK FOR A HUNDRED AND FIFTY-FOUR DAYS.** Entry 19 is a yard light, the maintenance is a bulb, and the bulb is out.
- **THE SLOT IN THAT COUNTER HAS HAD A CARD IN IT SINCE 20 FEBRUARY, SIXTY-EIGHT DAYS AS AT 29 APRIL, and nobody has taken it out and nobody has put it up.**
- **THE DRAWER UNDER THAT COUNTER IS A DRAWER WITH NO KEY ON IT AND NO LIST OF WHAT IS IN IT, and it is growing, and there is still no form in this borough on which anybody could write that these are not a series, and nine slips is a count of documents and about eleven people a day is a count of people and the two were never added and are never going to be.**
- **ON THE SECOND FLOOR THERE IS A TRAY WITH POST ON TOP OF SOMETHING IN IT, sixty-eight days, and a reference in the correct typeface, and nobody has opened it in front of a second person and nobody is going to.**
- **TWO CONDITIONS SIXTEEN INCHES APART ON A WALL THAT HAVE NEVER BEEN RECONCILED.**
- **THE AUTUMN COMPLIANCE REPORT STANDS WITH PARAGRAPH 4.11 ON IT IN FRONT OF FOUR HUNDRED AND TWENTY WOMEN AND WAS NEVER ANSWERED BY ANYBODY. The bench steriliser on level zero is still running. Its own strip goes in a drawer in that room about forty times a day and the hand that puts it there has not been found either and is not going to be. The frost warning came off on 2 April.**
- **THE BOARD STANDS AT SEVEN OF NINE, TWO CHAIRS, AND NO CHAIR OF THAT BODY AND THERE NEVER WAS.**
- **A METHOD THAT CAME OUT OF THAT COUNTER HAS TRAVELLED SIDEWAYS TO THREE PEOPLE AND FROM THEM TO AT LEAST THREE MORE, IT HAS TOUCHED A DISTRICT, IT IS IN NO DOCUMENT, IT CANNOT BE PRODUCED BY ANYBODY, AND JONAS MERCER IS THE PERSON WHO MADE IT CHEAP AND HAS ASKED ONE QUESTION ABOUT IT IN FOUR WEEKS AND WILL NOT GO AND ASK A SECOND.**
- **A MAN OF FORTY-FOUR HAS A FORTNIGHT SOMEWHERE IN THIS CITY WITH A BUCKET UNDER A TAP AND NOBODY IS ABLE TO ASK HIM WHETHER HE HAS GOT A COAT. A MAN OF FORTY-NINE CAN BE ASKED ONE QUESTION TOMORROW MORNING AND HAS NOT BEEN. A MAN OF FORTY-EIGHT HAS NOT BEEN ASKED AND IS NOT GOING TO BE. A MAN OF THIRTY-EIGHT IS FINDABLE NOW, TO THREE PEOPLE AND COUNTING, AND DOES NOT KNOW IT.**
- **A WOMAN OF THIRTY-FOUR IS IN ABOUT ELEVEN PEOPLE AND IN NO DOCUMENT AND CANNOT BE REACHED, and a hole in a record is the only honest thing anybody in this borough has produced about a person in nine months. A WOMAN OF FIFTY-FOUR IS FINDABLE AT A SHOPFRONT AND HAS NOT BEEN TOLD AND NOBODY HAS ASKED HER WHETHER SHE CAN AFFORD THAT, AND THIS BOROUGH HAS NO INSTRUMENT FOR ASKING.**
- **THE WEST-CLINIC GATE GOES UP AT SEVEN ON A SUNDAY BECAUSE TWO PEOPLE WHO ARE NOT ON ANYTHING WERE IN A ROOM WITH FOUR CHAIRS IN IT AT TEN O'CLOCK ON THE SATURDAY NIGHT, AND FOURTEEN OF THOSE SATURDAY NIGHTS HAVE NOW BEEN REACHED AND NOT ONE HAS A DATE ON IT, AND THE TERM IN FORCE IS THE FOURTEENTH AND EXPIRES AT SIX O'CLOCK ON SUNDAY THE THIRD OF MAY, AND THE FIFTEENTH IS READ AT TEN O'CLOCK ON SATURDAY THE SECOND OF MAY, AND THE ROTA HAS STILL NOT BEEN REBUILT AND CANNOT BE.**
- **THE CLAIMS BOX IS CLOSED AND IS EMPTY. THE SEALED ENVELOPE IS IN A DRAWER ON A LOWER FLOOR AND HAS NOT BEEN OPENED AND IS NOT GOING TO BE. THE WEIR IS ON NO LAYER AND CANNOT BE SEATED AND NOBODY ON IT HAS TOLD ANYBODY ANYTHING.**
- **A FINDING OF A BOROUGH SAFETY PANEL OF 23 APRIL 2014 WAS STATED OUT LOUD ONCE, IN A ROOM, IN DAYLIGHT, ON 28 JANUARY 2015, AND IS NOT RESOLVED AND MAY NOT BE OPENED AGAIN. WHAT IS LEFT OF IT IS ITS CONSEQUENCE, AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH HAS BEEN MADE TO LIVE UNDER THAT CONSEQUENCE YET AND NOBODY IS GOING TO BE. THE CLAIM TO OPEN A DOOR IN THIS VOLUME IS SPENT.**
- **WHAT A SERVICE FLOOR IS FOR, IN ONE SENTENCE, IN THE VOLUME'S OWN TERMS, AND WHO SAID IT, AND WHERE IT IS: `IT IS FOR THE PEOPLE THE BOROUGH CANNOT COUNT.` JONAS MERCER, Monday 27 April 2015, in a vestibule eleven feet by nine, in daylight, to a man of about fifty-six who is not on the agenda either, in a public minute with no names in it, Item 4. Nothing in this item requires any person to do anything.**

## 14. MINOR FIGURES INTRODUCED IN BATCH 0004, CARRIED FORWARD — DO NOT DUPLICATE

- **A MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY WITH A GALVANISED CAN OF ABOUT TEN LITRES**, not on any list, has never been in that vestibule before, came on 16 March 2015 and asked what was in the trough by the lock-ups on 2 February, was given the state in about eleven seconds in three halves, said `Then I will not put the drum in`, put the state back in the inside of his coat on 20 April, asked `Is that a receipt`, was told no and was told it cannot be paid, returned, filed or thrown away, and said `Then I am sorry I asked`, and she said nobody has ever said sorry to her at that counter and she would rather he did not make a thing of it. **HE IS NOT ON ANY LIST. HE IS THE ONLY OTHER PERSON IN THIS BOROUGH WHO KNOWS WHAT A SLIP FROM THAT COUNTER IS FOR, AND HE DOES NOT KNOW, AND HE IS CARRYING ONE.**
- **A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO WORKS IN A CANTEEN**, not on any list, has been in that corridor twice, told Jonas Mercer on the evening of 25 March that a counter down there gives you a date, that it is not strange any more, and that a girl on a till has had two of them off a landlord and said `oh, they all do that now`. **HE IS ONE OF THE THREE OTIENO GAVE IT TO — or he is downstream of one of the three; which it is is not established and nobody is going to establish it.**
- **A WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY WHO ASKED `Is the man who stands at the wall on a list`** at that counter on Friday 24 April 2015, was told no in about four seconds plus the second half, said `That's a shame` and went out into the street, and did not come back.
- **THE THREE OTIENO GAVE IT TO: a woman of about thirty-one at the depot, four years; a man of about fifty-five on the second floor of his building who does not work shifts; and a man about thirty he met once in a queue outside a hospital in the middle of February who has a daughter too and who wrote a date on the back of his hand.** None of them is on any list and none of them has written anything down.
- **ABU BAKAYOKO, 58** — his first slip of 26 January is seven weeks old in an inside pocket and has not been looked at since; he came on 2 and 13 February and has not come since; the man on his bowser is still paid on Fridays out of his own pocket. **DOREEN WHITLOCK, TREVOR NUNN, EZRA MBEKI, M. FURREAUX, Mr Hesketh, the woman of about fifty at the records counter who has been there eleven years and is NOT Nina Calder, the man of about fifty-six who is not a member, the woman of about sixty at the housing office answer book, the man of about fifty who came in on 6 March, the writer of the letter of 18 February, the writer of the letter of 6 February** — all carried forward unchanged.
- **BRAM OSEI, a porter at Saint Orra west**, the day bearer, has a refused Thursday on a board in his own handwriting with *no reason I can give* under a *no reason given* in somebody else's hand, has asked twice that the second line not be looked at, nobody looked at it in this batch, and **on 3 April 2015 he said, in a corridor, in daylight, unasked, three words: `You have not been down.` A porter is a person who can tell the difference between a man who is there and a man who is not, and that is now established in this account, and it was established by a man noticing on his own, and nobody has said anything to him about it and he has told nobody.**

## 15. WHAT IS STILL OPEN AND MAY NOT BE RESOLVED IN CHAPTERS 141–144

The Weir, the annex, the retainer, the two-refusal question and the day after, the burnt letter, the boy of nineteen, the map's margin, the blank line at the bottom of the form, the sealed envelope in the drawer on a lower floor, Margo Doré's pump, Dev Singh's empty-crossing clause, the Selah Mora log, the tin, the brass tag, the 11:00–13:30 gap, Bram Osei's Thursday, Priya Ranganathan's card, the unverified compliance week and paragraph 4.11, the man of forty-eight, the hand that puts the strip in the drawer, the roof void at Carrow Lane, the second of the pair that did not go into the register, the third and the fourth of the four differences between the two public records, the two conditions on the Old Ward board and the initials on the back of the enamel strip, the seventh shape, the lowland's missing seat, the letter of 18 February, the card in the post slot, the shelf, **and the reason he is not going to be in a room on 2 May.** **Nine hundred and forty is a number of times; nine hundred and eighty-six is a number of Wednesdays, and it appears exactly once in Chapters 131–140, in Chapter 0140, correctly labelled and in a sentence containing neither of the other two; fourteen is a difference between two public numbers and is on no instrument. None of the three is a number of people and none of them may be, and they have never been in the same sentence and must not be.** The claims box is closed and empty and the sealed envelope may not be opened or written into. **The forty minutes of 15 April 2014 is time until a family knew, belongs to a woman of eighty-four in bay six, and is not about a door; no 48-minute figure may ever be printed; the forty *hours* of 3 April 2014 is the gate being open and is a different number about a different thing; and no other figure of minutes exists anywhere in this borough. NOTHING WHATSOEVER HAPPENS ON WEDNESDAY 15 APRIL 2015, AND IT IS A WEDNESDAY.** No root reading, no root clause, no succession, no Iona, no Evan, no counter-amendment, no second signature, no Quiet Cut, no origin of the community-watch term, no three-line brass tokens — **those are Volumes 07, 09 and 15. Prue Calloway's location-of-record finding of 23 April 2014 was OPENED ONCE, IN A ROOM, IN DAYLIGHT, ON 28 JANUARY 2015, AND IT IS NOT RESOLVED, AND IT MAY NOT BE OPENED A SECOND TIME; what may be argued afterwards is its consequence, and nobody in this borough has been made to live under the consequence yet and nobody is going to be. The claim to open a door in this volume is spent.** **A person who is not counted is not a person who is helped, and Volume 03 may not refute that sentence in a chapter. Tomas Renn's question about the day after is unanswered and Batch 0005 may not resolve it and may not let anybody else manufacture it. The fifth items of 28 January 2015 may not be restated as a list, in any form, in any room, and may not go on a wall. The sentence of 13 March 2015 may not be restated. The nine words about a level of service are binding on every later batch. A counter may not answer anything about a gate, a rota, a compliance paragraph or a person. The shelf may not be added to the throughput. Nothing may be written down about the three people Otieno gave it to, or about which district one of them works in.**

**VOLUME 03 IS NOT CLOSED. IT RUNS TO CHAPTER 144. CHAPTER 0140 IS NOT THE VOLUME'S CLOSE AND SAYS SO. CHAPTERS 141–144 ARE THE NEXT PHASE AND HAVE NOT BEEN WRITTEN. NOTHING IN THIS BLOCK MAY BE CONTRADICTED BY THE VOLUME CLOSE WITHOUT A LINE HERE SAYING SO.**
